One report, two asks, split on purpose. **BUG-172 (P2, READY, root-caused, PIPELINE-SAFE):** a run of podcast mentions from the same episode stacks as N near-identical rows with N nodes on one date. Verified: `feed-view.tsx:219-226` gives every mention on an episode the same `sortDate`, `predicateRank` returns the same `7` for all of them so there is no tie-break, each renders its own `MentionRow` and node (lines 77, 415), and `context="timeline"` leads with the episode label (`mention-row.tsx:48-57`), so "FNRad #142" repeats down the column. The existing `groupMentionsByMoment` (`lib/mentions.ts:58`) does NOT solve it: it keys on timestamp plus excerpt to fold a story written once per subject, and on a person's timeline every row is already that person, so it folds nothing. The key needed is `episode_event_id`. `entity-mentions.tsx:60-66` has the identical stacking on place, brand, board and event pages and is folded into the same PR. Counts verified safe (`filterCounts.mentions` is the raw count, `filterCounts.all` deliberately excludes mentions, so the "Entry #N" celebration cannot regress); only the decade header `{grouped[decade].length} entries` moves, addressed in the brief. `/t/[slug]` renders no mentions and the episode views already group by moment, both listed as do-not-touch. Brief: `bugs/2026-08-18-timeline-mention-grouping.md`, 6 decisions, read-only pre-flight SQL, no migration. **BUG-173 (NOT-READY, feature, no brief):** the same report's second paragraph asks that mentions be placed by when the story happened rather than when it was told. `Mention` carries no such field (`types/index.ts:452-478`), so it needs an additive date column, an authoring surface in the transcript import and the editor modal, and a product call on what an undated mention does. ## How the loop works

0. **Reconcile (first thing each run).** Before reading any email, the triage task checks for build sessions that shipped since the last run by reading the git log on `main` (`git log main --since=...`, or the merged-PR list). It looks at every merged PR, not just bug ones: `BUG-NNN` ids mark bug sessions, `feat`/`fix`/`chore` subjects with a `(#NN)` mark feature and chore sessions. For each shipped session it: (a) moves any BUG ids from the **Queue** to **Shipped** with the PR number, (b) reconciles `bugs/SHIP-LOG.md` (flip the matching `status: pending` entry to `status: merged` with the PR number and squash sha; expand any `PENDING auto-stub` the SessionEnd hook left into a one-line summary; add an entry for any merged PR that has none, which is how feature sessions get recorded since they carry no `BUG-NNN`), (c) archives the matching `NEXT-SESSION.md` brief to `bugs/archive/<date>-<slug>.md`, and (d) resets `NEXT-SESSION.md` to "NO BUILD-READY BRIEF YET" (or to the next candidate cluster). This is what keeps the Queue, the brief, and the ship log from going stale; skipping it is how a shipped session gets re-handed as the "top item." Claude Code deliberately does NOT touch the **Shipped** section here, and only ever leaves SHIP-LOG entries as `pending`; this reconcile is the only thing that flips them and closes the loop. SHIP-LOG is append-only, so never rewrite earlier entries beyond the pending-to-merged flip.
1. **Capture.** A bug lands in Gmail (see Intake below). The bridge in `Operations/gmail-bug-attachments-bridge.md` auto-saves its attachments to Drive, then marks the email read, archives it out of the inbox, and files it under the `linestry/bugs` label. The inbox stays clear; triage still finds the emails by search.
2. **Triage.** A scheduled Cowork task runs each morning: reconciles shipped work (step 0), then reads new bug emails, reviews any attached screenshots from the Drive folder, dedupes against this file, assigns severity, clusters related reports, and updates the **Queue** below.
3. **Prep + auto-draft.** Each cluster gets a Claude Code mini-brief here (format at the bottom). Then the triage task auto-drafts a self-contained, build-ready brief for **every ready cluster** as a dated file in `bugs/<YYYY-MM-DD>-<slug>.md`, so a code session can start with no further writing. A cluster is "ready" when it is a bug-fix-shaped repair or polish job; clusters needing a strategic product call or a feature build are left flagged in the Queue ("needs decision" / "separate session") rather than drafted. When a ready cluster carries a product or design choice, the brief does NOT block: it picks a sensible recommended default and surfaces it in a "DECISIONS (review before building)" block at the top of the brief, with the alternative noted, so Jay can override before the session without anything waiting on him.
4. **Fix.** Jay opens `NEXT-SESSION.md` (it points at the lead brief and lists the rest), optionally overrides a flagged decision, and hands the cluster to Claude Code; Claude Code ships a PR that names the BUG ids in its title or commit message. The NEXT morning's reconcile (step 0) reads that PR and marks the bugs done here. The id naming is the signal that makes the reconcile automatic.

5. **Regenerate the readable dashboard.** As the final step of every run, after this file and `NEXT-SESSION.md` are saved, run `python3 bugs/render-queue.py`. It parses this file's Queue + Shipped sections (and the lead from `NEXT-SESSION.md`) and rewrites `bugs/bug-queue.html`, the styled snapshot Jay opens. It is stdlib-only, scrubs em/en dashes, and derives the group, counts, lead, and next-free-id automatically, so the HTML never drifts from this markdown. Do not hand-edit `bug-queue.html`; edit the markdown and re-run the script.

Visual review depends on the Gmail-to-Drive bridge being set up once. See `Operations/gmail-bug-attachments-bridge.md`. Until then, triage runs on email text only.

Manual run any time: just say "run bug triage" in a Cowork session.

---

## Intake (how bugs get in)

Three ways, all landing in the same pipeline:

- **In-app widget (shipped, PR #46):** signed-in users report from the avatar dropdown; logged-out visitors from the "Sign in" menu. Each report persists a `bug_reports` row and emails a structured `[Linestry Bug]` report to jay@lineage.community (with a PostHog replay link), so it flows through the bridge and triage like any other. This is now the primary intake.
- **Subject convention (manual, no setup):** email or forward to jay@lineage.community with `[Linestry Bug]` at the start of the subject line. The triage task searches for this.
- **Gmail label (optional, needs one-time setup):** create a `linestry/bugs` label in Gmail plus a filter that applies it to anything with `[Linestry Bug]` in the subject. The triage task will also pick up `label:linestry/bugs`.

**Connector note:** the daily triage task reads the connected Gmail account, which is jay@lineage.community. No extra setup needed for the automated read.

**What a good report includes** (paste this to advisors):

> Found a bug? Email jay@lineage.community with `[Linestry Bug]` in the subject. In the body tell me:
> 1. What you were trying to do
> 2. What happened instead
> 3. The page URL (copy from the address bar)
> 4. Roughly what time it happened (so we can find the session replay)
> 5. Phone or computer, and which browser
> A screenshot or screen recording helps a lot.

(The in-app widget captures 3 to 5 automatically, so reports through it already arrive complete. Shipped via PR #46.)

---

## Severity rubric

| Level | Definition | Examples | Target |
|---|---|---|---|
| **P0** | Data loss, auth broken, or page will not load. Blocks core use. | Cannot sign in, timeline wipes on save, white screen on load | Same-day |
| **P1** | Core flow broken but a workaround exists. | Add Story fails on first try but works on retry, tag does not save | Within 2 to 3 days |
| **P2** | Cosmetic, edge case, or polish. | Misaligned card on mobile, wrong date format, typo | Batch when convenient |

**Repro flag** on every item: `reproducible` (clear steps) or `needs-info` (vague, parked until clarified). `needs-info` items do not enter the active queue until someone can reproduce them or the reporter answers a follow-up.

### BUG-179: Your own timeline flips into the anonymous public view and asks you to claim your own profile  [P1] [needs-info on the trigger, root cause verified] (no image)
- Reported: August 31 (20:38 UTC) by `OWNER` (macOS Chrome 150, desktop 1851x936, `https://linestry.com/people/315a92ff-acef-404d-ae20-b6b3eb01f8ea`), 1 report via the in-app widget. Reporter flagged it "Urgent".
- Symptom, in the reporter's words: "not sure how I got into this sate of being asked to claim my profile. I think I was on my timeline, and then pressed the button to Create A Story."
- No image. The report carried no screenshot line, and a Drive search of "Linestry Bug Attachments" for `1a0598bb3e48245d` returned nothing, which is consistent with none being attached.
- Why the URL matters: `/people/<own-uuid>` IS the owner's own timeline. `src/app/people/[id]/page.tsx:217` returns `<OwnerTimelinePanel />` when `isAuthUser(activePersonId) && resolvedId === activePersonId`, and its own comment names this URL as the redirect target from `/me/timeline` and `/{community}/profile`. So the reporter was on the right page; the page decided he was a stranger on it.
- Root cause, mechanism 1 (verified against `f6ec7f8`): that owner/public branch is taken with no `authReady` gate. `authReady` is never referenced anywhere in `src/app/people/[id]/page.tsx`, and the only hold on the page is `if (!catalogLoaded)` at line ~203. The repo `CLAUDE.md` state section says plainly to wait for `authReady` before auth-gating UI. Until auth resolves, `activePersonId` is not the owner, the branch falls through to the read-only public view, and `showThatsMeAnon = !isAuth && !isCurrentUser && nodeIsClaimable` (line 281) renders the "Is this you? Claim this profile with your email" panel at line 509.
- Root cause, mechanism 2 (verified, and the better fit for a mid-session flip): `src/components/catalog-loader.tsx:118-135`. If `supabase.auth.getUser()` returns no uid for any reason, the loader calls `setActivePersonId("")` and then `setAuthReady(true)`. On `/people/<own-uuid>` that instantly demotes the signed-in owner to anonymous and swaps his timeline for the stranger view plus the claim CTA, and it stays that way until a reload. This matches "I was on my timeline, and then pressed the button" better than a cold-load race does, because it can fire at any moment in the session.
- Open question a session must answer first: which mechanism actually fired here, and whether the claim CTA that appeared was the anonymous one (`showThatsMeAnon`, line 509) or the signed-in one (`showThisIsMe`, line 486). The two point at different fixes. If it was `showThisIsMe`, then `activePersonId` was a valid auth id that was not `315a92ff`, and the real bug is an identity mismatch rather than an auth-readiness flip. The PostHog replay is the fastest way to settle it.
- Session replay: PostHog `S-49` (`?t@2` in the emailed link); email August 31, 2026 20:38 UTC. No image.
- Acceptance: a signed-in member on their own `/people/<own-uuid>` never sees a claim-this-profile CTA, and never sees the read-only stranger view of themselves, during hydration, after a transient auth read, or at any other point in the session. Diagnosis-first, and auth-adjacent, so HUMAN-RUN and excluded from auto-merge. Brief: `bugs/2026-09-01-owner-profile-claim-flip.md`.

### BUG-177: The "unverified" chip renders in three different visual treatments  [P2] [reproducible] (image reviewed)
- Reported: August 25 (14:45 UTC) by `R1` (iPhone Safari 26.4, 414x340, `/snowboarding/brands`), 1 report via the in-app widget
- Symptom: "The unverified tag appearance is different in the Feed section vs other sections."
- Image reviewed (`1a03961f1b489cc0__0__bug-screenshot.jpg`): the Brands catalog list at 414px. DC, Elan, Endeavor and Forum each carry a small outline "unverified" chip beside the brand name; Gnu, Jones and K2 carry none.
- Root cause (verified against `44fb3c5`): three forked treatments for one state. (1) The shared `UnverifiedBadge` (`src/components/ui/badge.tsx:13-19`) is a filled dark pill (`bg-amber-950/60 text-amber-400`) with a `◎` glyph, and its only feed appearance (`src/components/feed/post-card.tsx:687`) sits inside a `.postcard`, which per repo `CLAUDE.md` gotcha 7 forces light-theme tokens even in dark mode. So the dark pill lands on the one surface guaranteed to be light. (2) Five hand-rolled outline chips (`brands/page.tsx:79`, `places/page.tsx:57`, `events/page.tsx:110`, `boards/board-parts.tsx:369` and `:426`) all repeat the literal `text-[10px] text-amber-600 border border-amber-500/40`, no glyph, no fill. (3) A third palette again at `src/app/claim/[token]/page.tsx:217`. The underlying state (`community_status === "unverified"`) is consistent everywhere; only the rendering forked.
- Session replay: PostHog `S-48`; email August 25, 2026 14:45 UTC; image `1a03961f1b489cc0__0__bug-screenshot.jpg` reviewed.
- Acceptance: one shared badge, legible in both themes and specifically inside a `.postcard`; the hand-rolled class literal is gone from `src`. Brief: `bugs/2026-08-27-unverified-badge-consistency.md` (clustered with BUG-178).

### BUG-178: A rider's claims show "unverified" on some entries and not others, with nothing to explain why  [P2] [reproducible] (image reviewed; labelling, not a defect)
- Reported: August 25 (14:37 UTC) by `R1` (iPhone Safari 26.4, 414x305, `/snowboarding/feed`), 1 report via the in-app widget
- Symptom: "Geoff Peterson added a bunch of Rode At to his timeline. In the feeds area for Geoff. Some are tagged unverified while others are not. Just double checking if this right?"
- Image reviewed (`1a0395ae6713e033__0__bug-screenshot.jpg`): four consecutive Geoff Peterson feed cards at 414px. "Lyon Mountain, Alberta, Canada / RODE AT 2026 / Self-reported / unverified" carries the badge; "Lake Louise Ski Resort, AB, Canada / RODE AT 1990 / Self-reported" does not. Same rider, same predicate, same confidence.
- Diagnosis (verified against `44fb3c5`): the behaviour is CORRECT and the labelling is what fails. `post-card.tsx:622-629` derives `isUnverified` from `userEntities` and checks `community_status`, so the badge is a status on the PLACE, not on the rider's claim. Lake Louise is an established catalog place; Lyon Mountain was member-added and still unverified. The problem is that the chip sits in the metadata row immediately after `ConfidenceBadge` ("Self-reported"), which IS a judgement about the claim, so two chips on two different axes sit side by side with nothing to tell them apart. A careful reader reads it as the system arbitrarily doubting some of his entries.
- Session replay: PostHog `S-48`; email August 25, 2026 14:37 UTC; image `1a0395ae6713e033__0__bug-screenshot.jpg` reviewed.
- Acceptance: the chip names what it is judging (recommended default `unverified place` / `unverified brand` / `unverified board` / `unverified event`); claims pointing at established catalog entities still carry no badge; `ConfidenceBadge` untouched. Carries a copy decision, defaults recommended in the brief. Brief: `bugs/2026-08-27-unverified-badge-consistency.md` (clustered with BUG-177).

### BUG-152: Community date-verification ask + admin cannot edit story dates/connections  [enhancement] [feature] [needs-decision] (no image)
- Reported: July 27 (04:27 UTC) by Jay (OWNER, desktop Chrome 1456x909, `/snowboarding/stories`), 1 report via the in-app widget (widget notes "Screenshot: Not provided", so no Drive search needed)
- Ask: two related asks in one report, prompted by two of Sean's posts with uncertain dates. (1) "Need a fun community way to help each other figure out dates ... Like a question for the community to help answer and verify": a community mechanic where uncertain story dates become a question others help answer and verify. (2) "Is there a way for admin to change the dates or connections on stories": an admin capability question.
- Code facts (verified this run against the live repo): the answer to (2) is NO today. `src/app/api/stories/route.ts` PATCH verifies `author_id === user.id` (owner-only); only DELETE carries the editor/moderator takedown branch (is_editor, PR #129 pattern). The StoryCard edit menu (`src/components/feed/story-card.tsx`) is owner-only too. So admin story-edit is a real tooling gap, not a bug.
- Why NOT-READY: part (1) is a feature+design build (crowd date-verification; adjacent to the PB-012 question-of-the-week mechanic and to the claims `confidence`/`approximate` fields as the verification substrate). Part (2) is admin tooling (extend stories PATCH with the same is_editor branch DELETE has, plus an editor edit affordance on StoryCard); it clusters with the admin-parity items BUG-093 (board edit), BUG-133 (place edit), and BUG-025 (unclaimed-member tooling) as a candidate admin-editing session.
- Session replay: PostHog `S-36` (offset 57s); email July 27, 2026 04:27 UTC; no image.
- Next step: Jay decision. Recommended split: (a) if wanted before the push round, queue the small admin story-edit parity piece as its own feature brief (editor branch on stories PATCH + editor ⋯ edit affordance); (b) park the community date-verification mechanic as a feature-spec item, likely folded into PB-012 scoping in September.

### BUG-149: Sign-in (magic-link) email shows a fallback-font wordmark and no logo  [P2] [reproducible] (image in Drive)
- Reported: July 5 (04:25 UTC) by the cy_3 test account (R3, iPad Safari 820x1048, requested from `/people/cy_3`), 1 report (screenshot `19f308660eb64af7__0__bug-screenshot.jpg` in Drive)
- Symptom: "Signing in with email and request an email link. The email image has the Linestry work mark using old font and missing new logo."
- Surface / suspected area: the transactional email header `src/lib/emails/shared-header.ts` (`emailHeaderHtml()`, used by the magic-link email `src/app/api/auth/magic-link/route.ts` plus reset-password, claim, comment, tag emails) embeds the v2 monogram as an INLINE SVG and sets the wordmark font to `'Geologica',Arial`. Apple Mail and most email clients do not render inline SVG (so the logo reads as "missing") and cannot load Geologica (so the wordmark falls back to Arial, read as "old font"). Fix: replace the inline SVG with a hosted PNG raster of the v2 monogram (served from linestry.com) so it renders in email; accept the Arial wordmark fallback or raster the wordmark too. Affects ALL transactional emails once shared-header is fixed.
- Session replay: PostHog `S-33` (offset 225s); screenshot `19f308660eb64af7__0__bug-screenshot.jpg`; email July 5, 2026 04:25 UTC.
- Acceptance: the sign-in email renders the v2 monogram (as a hosted image) in Apple Mail / Gmail; the header reads as branded, not a broken image. Brief: `bugs/2026-07-05-email-mark-render.md`.

A copy trim is subjective and touches brand voice, so NOT auto-drafted; needs Jay's wording (and ideally a brand-voice pass). Candidate for a launch copy session rather than a bug fix.

Its own message lists: drop the repeated "scattered" from the intro, collapse the triple "weave / woven / bring together" down to one use, **cut the repeated "our stories" across the blocks**, and replace the long snowboarding-focus sentence with a punchy line. ### BUG-133: No "Edit" affordance on an individual place page; admin Dataset Editor edits places but only core fields  [P2] [reproducible] (no image; feature gap, sibling to BUG-093; premise partly stale)
- Reported: July 3 by Jay (OWNER), session-watching notes ("There is not a way to edit 'Places' even as an admin") (no image)
- Symptom: as reported, there is no way to edit a Place, even as an admin.
- Premise check (Group E): places ARE editable in the admin Dataset Editor Places tab (name / type / region / country) via `updateCatalogEntity("places")` — `src/app/admin/page.tsx:879-920`; `POST /api/admin` allows `update` on the `places` table (`src/app/api/admin/route.ts:5,46-48`). So the reported premise is partly stale: a limited admin edit exists.
- Real gaps: (1) no editor/admin "Edit" affordance on the public place detail page `src/app/(community)/[community]/places/[id]/page.tsx` — direct sibling of BUG-093 (boards); (2) the admin Places edit omits `website`, `description`, and `first_snowboard_year`, which the create flow captures (`add-entity-modal.tsx:149-162`). There is no `edit-place-modal` (only edit-claim / edit-event / edit-profile exist).
- Surface / suspected area: place detail page + the existing `updateCatalogEntity` / `POST /api/admin` editor path (`requireEditor`-gated). Reuse the BUG-093 board-edit pattern.
- Acceptance (once built): an editor/admin viewing a place page can edit its catalog fields inline (all creation fields: name, type, region, country, website, description, first snowboard year), reusing the admin update path, with no edit affordance shown to non-editors.

### BUG-131: Adding multiple comma-separated brands during signup creates one brand  [feature] [reproducible] (no image; feature; keep the add-a-brand flow simple)
- Reported: July 3 by Jay (OWNER), session-watching notes (Ken Achenbach invite) (no image)
- Symptom: Ken entered his brands as a comma-separated list ("Winterstick, Amplid, Burton, AK, Analog, Green Clothing, Beaver Wax") in the signup "What brand do you ride?" step; they all saved as a single brand with that literal comma-joined name.
- Surface / suspected area: the onboarding brand picker (`SearchableEntityPicker` in `src/components/onboarding/onboarding-flow.tsx:199-206`) takes a single query string and its "+ Add … as a new brand" affordance opens `AddEntityModal entityType="org"` (line 169-178), which creates ONE org named with the full query. No comma-splitting exists on any add path.
- Fix (keep it simple, per Jay): when the brand input contains commas, split on commas and create one brand per non-empty token; more detail can be added later.
- Acceptance: entering comma-separated brand names in the signup brand step creates one brand entity per name. FEATURE (cross-listed in `features/feature-queue.md`).

### BUG-130: "Add a brand" during onboarding defaults the entity Type to a non-brand value and exposes the full type dropdown  [P2] [reproducible] (no image; root cause verified)
- Reported: July 3 by Jay (OWNER), session-watching notes (Ken Achenbach invite) (no image)
- Symptom: in the signup "What brand do you ride?" step, the "Add a brand" modal showed the default Type as "event series" instead of "brand".
- Surface / suspected area: the onboarding brand step opens `AddEntityModal entityType="org"` WITHOUT passing `initialOrgType` (`src/components/onboarding/onboarding-flow.tsx:169-178`). `AddEntityModal` defaults `orgType` to `initialOrgType ?? "shop"` (`src/components/ui/add-entity-modal.tsx:101`) and renders the full `ORG_TYPES` select — brand / shop / team / magazine / event-series (`add-entity-modal.tsx:394-404`). Current code defaults to "shop" (Ken saw "event series", likely an older deploy), but either way the type is not pinned to "brand" and the full dropdown is exposed in a context that is explicitly about brands.
- Fix: pass `initialOrgType="brand"` from the onboarding brand step, and hide/lock the Type selector when the context is explicitly "What brand do you ride?".
- Acceptance: adding a brand from the onboarding brand step creates an org with `org_type = "brand"` by default; the type selector is not a source of confusion in that context.

### BUG-120: Compare button on a rider page does not prepopulate that rider  [P2] [reproducible] (no image; root cause verified)

### BUG-108: "Earned Today" bar reads 0/20 while the breakdown shows a "Showing up +1 token" was earned  [P2] [reproducible] (image near-blank; diagnosis-first; small decision)
- Reported: June 25 (00:43 UTC) by Jay (OWNER, desktop Chrome 1468x847, `/account/membership`), 1 report (image attached but near-blank/not text-extractable)
- Symptom: the "Earned Today" bar shows "0/20 tokens" while the expanded breakdown lists "Showing up +1 token". The visit token is earned but the headline reads zero, looking like it did not register.
- Visual review: screenshot `19efc3b6c7474b9f__0__bug-screenshot.jpg` present in Drive but ~10KB and not text-extractable; proceeding from the report text.
- Surface / suspected area: PARTLY INTENDED. `src/components/ui/daily-token-chip.tsx` renders `{earned}/{cap}` from `total_earned_today`; the daily "Showing up" (`daily_visit`) token sits OUTSIDE the content cap by design (line ~166 "Showing up and onboarding sit outside the cap"; line ~136 comment), so `total_earned_today` is 0 when only the visit token was earned. Decision: keep the cap meter content-only but make the visit token legible at the headline so it never reads 0 when a token was earned (recommended: a small "+1 showing up" chip by the number, or always-visible visit line). Diagnosis-first with a small UX decision.
- Session replay / visual: PostHog replay `S-21` (offset 2079 seconds); screenshot `19efc3b6c7474b9f__0__bug-screenshot.jpg`; email June 25, 2026 00:43 UTC.
- Acceptance: on a day with only the visit token earned, the membership token surface does not read as "nothing earned"; the cap meter math is unchanged. Client-only, no migration. Brief: `bugs/2026-06-25-earned-today-visit-token.md`.

### BUG-095: Board catalog tiles on a brand drill-down do not show the large cover image (the smaller brand-card thumbnail does)  [P2] [reproducible] (image reviewed; diagnosis-first)
- Reported: June 21 (03:07 UTC) by Cory (R1, iPhone 414x750, `/snowboarding/boards?brand=Arbor`), 1 report (image reviewed)
- Symptom: on the Arbor brand drill-down the large board cover images do not show on the product tiles, even though the smaller thumbnail (on the brand index card / when you click into the board) does show.
- Visual review: screenshot `19ee8263cb8d2608__0__bug-screenshot.jpg` shows the Arbor drill-down 2010s group with "Arbor A-Frame '10", "Arbor Coda '10", "Arbor Westmark '10" tiles rendering with no cover image (name + year + the add affordance only), confirming the large tile covers are missing while the brand index card cover strip resolves.
- Repro: open `/snowboarding/boards`, drill into Arbor; the large product tiles show no cover, but the brand-index card cover (and the board detail page) do.
- Surface / suspected area: DIAGNOSIS-FIRST. The brand drill-down product tiles resolve covers through the shared `BoardCover` / `useBoardImage` path; the brand index card cover strip and the board detail page resolve through a different path (the bulk `GET /api/board-image/list` community-image map and/or the live `/api/board-image` Serper fetch). Two leading hypotheses: (1) the drill-down tiles depend on the auto-cover (Serper) path, which per the PR #108 note only resolves where `SERPER_API_KEY` is set (it may not be set in Vercel prod), so boards without a community/stored image show the grey-mark fallback on tiles; (2) the drill-down tiles are not being fed the bulk community-image map the brand index uses, so a community/stored image that the smaller thumbnail resolves is not passed to the large tile. Trace which cover source the drill-down tiles use vs the brand index card, and confirm whether `SERPER_API_KEY` is set in Vercel prod. Could be config (env) rather than code, so HUMAN-RUN. Brief at `bugs/2026-06-22-boards-arbor-covers.md`.
- Session replay / visual: PostHog replay `S-18` (offset 585 seconds); screenshot `19ee8263cb8d2608__0__bug-screenshot.jpg`; email June 21, 2026 03:07 UTC.
- Acceptance: the large board cover image renders on the brand drill-down product tiles wherever it renders on the brand index card / board detail page (same source); boards genuinely without any image still fall back to the grey mark.

### BUG-076: `/snowboarding/feed` shows every individual person-tag as its own card, burying stories  [P2] [reproducible] (no image; needs design decision)
- Reported: June 18 (00:38 UTC) by Jay (OWNER, desktop 1585x843, `/snowboarding/feed`), 1 report (no image)
- Symptom: each person added to an event or story posts its own feed card, which floods the feed and buries actual stories. Jay wants per-person tags for the same event accumulated onto one card.
- Re-reported August 13, 2026 by Jay (OWNER, desktop 1271x965, `/snowboarding/feed`; PostHog replay `S-39`; no image). A new member (Geoff) checking off every place he rode buries the good stories. Adds a design refinement to the original ask: surface a single "New Member" card and GROUP the simple, low-signal entry types together (by type), not only per-event tag accumulation. Corroborates the original; still needs a design decision, not auto-drafted.
- Repro: open `/snowboarding/feed` after several riders have been tagged to one event; note one card per tag.
- Surface / suspected area: this is a feed-aggregation design + build, not a simple repair. Decide the grouping rule (group claims by event/object within a window onto a single "N riders added to X" card) before scoping. Touches the feed read + card rendering (`src/components/feed/feed-view.tsx`, the claim-card path). Related to the recently-added feed sort (BUG-055, shipped PR #88). NEEDS A DESIGN DECISION; not auto-drafted; queue as a feature/design session.
- Session replay / visual: PostHog replay `S-13` (offset 8773 seconds); no screenshot; email June 18, 2026 00:38 UTC.
- Acceptance (once designed): the community feed groups low-signal simple entries (per-event/place person-tags, new-member place check-offs) into accumulated or typed cards (for example a single "New Member" card and "N riders added to X" cards) so stories are not buried.
- **THIRD REPORT, September 3, 2026, and the first one that arrives with a proposed shape.** From the in-app feedback widget, sent by Jay (signed-in member) from `/snowboarding/feed` on desktop Chrome at 1617x942, September 2 at 22:12 UTC. Notable: it came through the `[Linestry Idea]` lane, not the bug lane, so it is filed as an idea bullet in `features/feature-queue.md` (BUG-076 entry there, expanded) and is recorded here as a +1 rather than a new id. Verbatim: "Individual cards for each new resort or connection by one person is filling the feed. The focus should be on stories, with the entries grouped together where possible (Geoff added X places to their timeline)." Replay `S-52`; no screenshot and none claimed. Two new facts on top of the June and August reports. (1) The grouping key Jay reaches for is the PERSON, "Geoff added X places to their timeline", not the event, which is what the original entry and the acceptance line above were both written around. Those are different cards and the brief that eventually gets written has to pick. (2) He states the ranking intent outright, that stories are the point of the feed and claims are context, which is the product call that has kept this parked since June. Same reporter, same surface, same member (Geoff) triggering it, now three times across eleven weeks: this is the most-repeated open item in the queue.
- **Implementation precedent worth copying rather than inventing (noted September 3):** `src/components/feed/mention-episode-group.tsx` plus the grouping helper in `feed-view.tsx` already collapse many rows into one header card for podcast mentions, shipped as BUG-172 (PR #197) and BUG-175 (PR #209), and the header-only collapsed treatment BUG-175 landed is very close to what is being asked for here. The feed entry list and its `authorForClaim` resolution live in `src/app/(community)/[community]/feed/page.tsx`, last touched by PR #211.
- STILL NOT AUTO-DRAFTED, and the reason is unchanged: this is a feed-aggregation build with four open product decisions (grouping key, threshold, how a group counts for ordering, copy), not a repair. FLAG: needs decision (Jay). It is the strongest candidate in the queue for conversion into a feature brief in an attended session.

### BUG-071: Bug-report popup buttons (Cancel / Send / Attach) do not register taps on mobile  [P2] [reproducible] (diagnosis-first; meta: feedback channel)
- Reported: June 17 (06:03 UTC) by Cory (R1, iPhone 414x750, `/snowboarding/events`), 1 report (no image; Screenshot not provided)
- Symptom: the in-app bug-reporting popup has trouble registering the Cancel and Send buttons and the attach-screenshot button on mobile.
- Repro: signed in (iPhone Safari), open the report-bug widget, type a note, and try tapping Attach / Send / Cancel; taps are unreliable.
- Surface / suspected area: DIAGNOSIS-FIRST. `src/components/ui/report-bug-modal.tsx` (Cancel `onClose` line ~241, Send `handleSend` line ~248 gated by `canSend = note.trim().length > 0`, Attach `fileInputRef.current?.click()` line ~230). Note: Send is intentionally disabled until a note is typed, which can read as "not registering" if the user expects it live, but Cancel and Attach are always enabled, so unreliable taps on those point at a touch/overlay issue (an overlay layer intercepting taps, a too-small mobile hit target, or a stacking/`pointer-events` problem). This widget is the feedback channel, so worth a quick mobile-Safari touch-target pass. Recently shipped overlay-positioning work (BUG-047/048/049, PR #80) is adjacent. Standalone brief `bugs/2026-06-17-bug-report-widget-buttons.md`.
- Session replay / visual: PostHog replay `S-11` (offset 1720s); no image found (Screenshot not provided); email June 17, 2026 06:03 UTC.
- CORROBORATING REPORT (June 19 03:43 UTC, Cory, iPhone 414x494, `/me/settings/notifications`, no image): "Sometimes I cannot press the cancel button when in the report a bug popup." Same widget, same Cancel-tap unreliability on mobile; pre-fix duplicate of BUG-071, not a new id. PostHog replay `S-14` (offset 6926 seconds). Reinforces the touch-target / overlay-intercept hypothesis.
- CORROBORATING REPORT (June 21 00:07 UTC, Cory, iPhone 414x750, `/people/jay_balmer`, screenshot in Drive): "Sometimes where you report a bug back to back in succession the report a bug popup cannot be closed." Same widget; specifically the back-to-back (reopen-after-send) case where the popup will not close, which points at a stale scroll-lock / overlay state not resetting between consecutive opens (the shared `useBodyScrollLock` ref count or the modal-open flag not clearing on the first close). Pre-fix duplicate of BUG-071, not a new id. PostHog replay `S-17` (offset 1143 seconds). Strengthens the case for a state-reset fix alongside the touch-target pass.
- CORROBORATING REPORT (July 4 03:41 UTC, Cory in5thgear, iPhone 414x750, `/people/cory_yip`, no image): "The Send button becomes very hard to press and can then accidentally close. Other bug is once the bug is sent and you try to report another bug the popup is frozen in a sending... state and screen is not tappable. You have to back out using the browser back button." Same widget; adds a NEW frozen-on-reopen symptom (the Send/sending state does not reset between consecutive reports, leaving the second open stuck and the page untappable, matching the June 21 back-to-back note). Pre-fix duplicate of BUG-071, not a new id. PostHog replay `S-28` (offset 2155 seconds). Strongly reinforces the state-reset (sending flag + scroll-lock not clearing) half of the fix alongside the touch-target pass.
- Acceptance: on mobile Safari the report-bug popup's Cancel, Attach, and Send (once a note is entered) all respond reliably to a single tap; hit targets are adequately sized; no overlay intercepts taps; AND after sending a report the modal fully resets so a second report opens clean (no stuck "sending..." state, screen tappable, no browser-back needed).

### BUG-063: Tapping to select a year on the collective timeline breaks after scrolling  [P2] [reproducible] (diagnosis-first; interaction)
- Reported: June 17 (00:41 UTC) by Cory (R1), 1 report (no image; Screenshot not provided)
- Symptom: tapping the collective timeline to select a year works at first, but after scrolling the selected year advances forward on its own and tapping to select a year becomes unreliable.
- Repro: signed in (iPhone Safari, 414x750), on `/snowboarding/collective` tap a year (works), scroll, and observe the selection drift forward and tap-to-select degrade.
- Surface: `src/app/(community)/[community]/collective/page.tsx` (the custom year/decade scrubber, not the shared timeline player).
- Suspected area: DIAGNOSIS-FIRST. The page has a `scrollRef` (line ~237) and an "auto-select best year/decade when panel is empty" effect (line ~287). Lead hypothesis: a scroll-driven setter (onScroll or an IntersectionObserver) competes with the explicit tap-select and overrides the tapped year after a scroll. Secondary: the auto-select effect re-running during scroll, a stale closure in the tap handler, or touch/scroll offset interplay. Trace the year-setting handlers before changing code. Brief auto-drafted (diagnosis-first) at `bugs/2026-06-17-collective-tap-year.md`; human-run recommended (real-device verification).
- Session replay / visual: PostHog replay `S-09` (offset 892s); no image found (Screenshot not provided); email June 17, 2026 00:41 UTC.
- Acceptance: tapping a year selects it and the selection stays put while scrolling; the year does not advance on its own; tap-to-select stays reliable after scrolling; decade mode and desktop unaffected.

### Deferred (product decision)

- **BUG-173: place a podcast mention in time by when the story happened, not when it was told** (NEW August 18, feature, needs a product call and a schema change). Reported August 18 (16:46 UTC) as the second half of the BUG-172 report, from `/people/sean_spud_balmer`, replay `S-45`: "I was also expecting the mentions to be spread by when the stories happened in time, not just when they were told. That is big feature :)". Jay flagged the size himself. Why it is not a fix: `src/types/index.ts:452-478` shows a mention carries only `timestamp_seconds` (an offset into the media) and a joined `episode.start_date`, so today `feed-view.tsx:224` has literally nothing else to sort on. Shipping this needs (1) an additive "when this happened" date on `mentions`, partial-date tolerant like `story_date`, (2) an authoring surface to fill it (the transcript import at `/admin/podcast/import` and the `MentionEditorModal`, plus a call on whether the import can infer a year from the excerpt), (3) a product decision on what a mention with no such date does: stay on the episode date, drop off the timeline, or sit in an "undated" group, and (4) a decision on whether the mention then reads as evidence about a moment (which starts to overlap claims and stories) rather than as an audio pointer. Not auto-drafted. Adjacent to BUG-172, which ships the presentation fix on the episode date and should land first.

- **Enable the public Stack by default for every member** (raised August 15 by Jay inside the BUG-160 report; deliberately split out of that brief). Needs a Jay decision on two counts: it turns on a PUBLIC surface for members who never opted in (a privacy call, not a bug fix), and switching existing members over is a backfill `UPDATE` on pre-existing `profiles` rows, which is GATED under the risk gate. The discoverability half of the same report is build-ready as BUG-160; this half builds only once Jay says yes. If it goes ahead, the natural shape is: flip the default for NEW profiles at creation, decide separately whether to backfill existing ones, and pair it with the "suggested starter" stack already in `/me/public-view` so a default-on Stack is never empty.

- **BUG-174: only some profiles show the Stack view button** (NEW August 20; **CONVERTED TO READY the same day** in a live session: Jay resolved the parked public-Stack-default decision. Calls locked: same visibility rules as the main timeline, auto-derived starter of the 3 most recent public timeline items until the member curates, backfill everyone non-archived, no rollout comms needed; the RENAME was resolved the same day: the member-facing pair is "Mini / Full" (compact view = Mini, full timeline = Full; copy-only, internal identifiers keep `stack`), folded into the brief as Decision 4. Build-ready brief: `bugs/2026-08-20-public-stack-default-on.md`. GATED profiles backfill pre-approved in the brief; apply with the full ship-sequence printout). Reported August 20 (06:47 UTC) from `/people/sean_spud_balmer` by an anonymous signed-out desktop session at 1271x965 (replay `S-46`, ~27s; same session as the BUG-175 report, so almost certainly Jay reviewing while logged out): "Only some profiles show the button to see the 'Stack' view (which needs a better name), on others the button is not there." Code-verified: the Stack/Timeline toggle on `/people/[id]` renders only when the viewed profile has `public_timeline_enabled === true` AND a `public_slug` (`src/app/people/[id]/page.tsx:148-162,302-313`), so the inconsistency is the opt-in gate working as designed. The real resolution is the bullet directly above (enable the public Stack by default for every member), which Jay already leaned toward on August 15 and which was sequenced behind BUG-159; BUG-159 shipped August 17, so that sequencing gate is GONE and the decision is ready to convert whenever Jay wants the rollout brief. NEW second decision this report adds: what to call the surface member-facing instead of "Stack" (candidates to spar in a live session; touches the toggle, `/me/public-view`, and the BUG-160 "Set up my Stack" links). Not auto-draftable: privacy default flip + backfill UPDATE on pre-existing `profiles` rows (GATED) + a naming call.

- **BUG-169: the membership page needs repositioning, not a repair** (NEW August 18, needs Jay's wording). Reported August 18 (16:26 UTC) from `/membership`, mobile 402x812, replay `S-43`. Jay dictated it, so the transcript is rough ("It's not really owning a sheriff history" is almost certainly "it's not really owning a shared history", and "Ministry" is the speech-to-text rendering of "linestry"). The ask is clear enough: make the page straightforward about what membership IS, in this order, membership supports the project, membership has privileges and benefits, membership includes participating in ownership if this goes somewhere, membership extends into other communities as they launch, plus a line about backing a startup platform in its first year and a "want to talk more, reach out" close. This is positioning and brand voice on the page that asks for money, so it needs Jay's own words or a brand-voice pass, not a mechanical fix. Sibling of **BUG-135** (landing copy too wordy); the two should probably be one launch copy session. Cross-check anything written here against the live equity-offer terms (the 100,000-share pool, snapshot noon PT April 30 2027) so the page cannot overpromise. Not auto-drafted.

- **BUG-170: the riders list should surface the riders you are actually connected to** (NEW August 18, feature, needs a product decision). Reported August 18 (16:33 UTC) by Jay from `/people`, desktop 1411x965, replay `S-44`: "I expect to see more riders in my timeline. Not sure what the rules are, but I have tagged many riders in my stories that I expected to see somehow. To see all the riders I'm connected with from shared Stories / points in the timelines. Want it sorted by connection type/group instead of membership type, leave the membership type to the card." Two asks: (1) a connections-derived view of riders (people you share a story, place, event or board with) rather than the flat catalog directory, and (2) grouping by connection type instead of membership tier, with tier demoted to the card. Both are new information architecture on a launch-facing surface, and the connection scoring already exists in `src/lib/connection-summary.ts` plus the `/me/connections` route, so the real question is whether this is a new view, a re-sort of `/people`, or a merge of `/people` and `/me/connections`. Adjacent to **BUG-076** (feed buries stories under individual person-tags) and to **BUG-114** (filter persistence). Needs Jay to pick the shape before anything is drafted. Not auto-drafted.

- **BUG-171: lifecycle email, abandoned-signup nudge plus a weekly digest from Jay** (NEW August 18, ops/feature, needs a sending policy; **+1 August 20 via the new idea lane**: Jay again asked for "Email to all members and pending members - like a quick newsletter of what is going on, and challenge/focus to add stories and connections. Connected to the podcast stories and riders - focus for the week." Repeat ask within 48 hours; the weekly-digest half is the part with rising signal, and the podcast-week tie-in gives it a concrete first issue). Reported August 18 (15:35 UTC), explicitly filed as a note to self: "we may need to send a personalized email to people who have started, but not completed account set up... a regular email from Myself that sort of summarizes highlights of the week or things we're looking for people to contribute." Two separate builds. The nudge needs a definition of "started but not completed" (an `auth.users` row with no `profiles` row, or a profile with `ftue_completed` false), a send window, a cap, and it MUST check `isEmailSuppressed` against `email_suppressions` like every other notification path. The weekly digest is closer to a newsletter than a product feature and may belong outside the app entirely for now, hand-sent or through a mail tool, given the current member count. Recommended split: scope the nudge as a small feature brief if wanted before the push round, and hand-send the digest until there is enough weekly activity to summarize. Not auto-drafted.

- **Collective "browse by year and category, then jump to that list"** (NEW August 18, companion to BUG-164, feature). The second half of the 15:49 UTC collective report, deliberately excluded from the BUG-164 brief, which ships only the missing intro and context. This half is a new navigation affordance on `/[community]/collective` and overlaps the open **BUG-117** (the collective does not put the viewer's own years in the default frame) and **BUG-063** (year selection breaks after scrolling). Those two should probably be fixed before a new browse-and-jump interaction is layered on top. Not auto-drafted.

- **BUG-163: catalog management and delegated catalog editors** (NEW August 18, needs a Jay decision; **Jay marked it low priority himself in the report**). Reported August 18 (04:18 UTC) by OWNER from `/snowboarding/boards/Sims_Undertaker_2023` (desktop Chrome 1411x965; replay `S-41`, offset ~59s): "No way to filter or manage the catalog. As an admin, I'd like to give select people the power to edit and manage the boards. Maybe a quick feedback system for someone to mark an issue, name, board, year." Three distinct asks in one report, none of them a repair: (1) filtering and bulk management on the catalog surfaces, (2) delegated per-domain editor permissions, which today is a single binary `profiles.is_editor` covering the whole `/admin` tree, and (3) a lightweight "flag an issue with this entry" affordance so members can report a wrong name or year without editor rights. Ask (3) is the cheapest and probably the highest value at this stage, and it has a proven pattern to copy in `tag_reports` plus `/admin/tag-queue` from PB-009 Phase 3. Ask (2) is a permissions model change and should not be improvised. Adjacent to the already-queued **BUG-093** (no editor Edit affordance on an individual board page) and **BUG-025** (admin surface for unclaimed members). Recommended split: scope (3) as a small feature brief if wanted before the push round, fold (1) into a catalog-pass feature session, and park (2) until there is more than one editor. Not auto-drafted.

## Shipped

Shipped August 28, 2026 via PR [#211](https://github.com/jaybalmer/lineage/pull/211) (`f6ec7f8`, BUG-176; branch `auto/bugfix-20260828-0801`; AUTONOMOUS 05:00 pipeline run, its fourth ship in nine days; no migration; tsc clean). Built from `bugs/2026-08-27-feed-rider-name.md` on all four recommended DECISIONS defaults, one file only:
- BUG-176: claim cards on `/[community]/feed` could paint with the rider's name silently missing. The cause was a catalog-load race, not missing data: `authorForClaim` resolves names only from the unpersisted, async `catalog.people`, and the feed was the one public community surface with no `catalogLoaded` gate. The render gate now reads `loading || (!catalogLoaded && !catalogError)`, so the list stays behind the existing loading state until both the fetch and the catalog land; the `catalogError` half is a deliberate deviation so a failed catalog fetch degrades instead of hanging on a permanent loader. A claim whose subject resolves to nobody in a fully loaded catalog (archived profile, or out of community scope) now renders a neutral unlinked "A rider" fallback, and `href` is emitted only when a real `display_name` resolves. Story cards untouched.
- NOTE: unattended pipeline session, so no browser smoke was run; the Slow 3G hard-reload check and a signed-out look at 414px are owed at review (the signed-out path is the stated regression risk of D2).

Shipped August 18, 2026 via PR [#193](https://github.com/jaybalmer/lineage/pull/193) (`30b7c1f`, BUG-168 / BUG-166; branch `fix/account-switch-and-ftue-entry-BUG-168-166`; attended session; no migration; tsc clean):
- BUG-168: the persisted Zustand store used one browser-wide key (`lineage-store-v2`) with no owner stamp, and SIGNED_OUT cleared only two slices, so a second account signing in on the same browser inherited the first account's membership tier, founding badge, member number and token counts. Fixed by keying the persisted store per owner (the `seen-celebrations.ts` uid-keyed pattern) and clearing the full identity surface on sign-out.
- BUG-166: "Start Your Timeline" dropped a signed-out visitor straight onto a bare onboarding question with no context and no exit. `/intro` (PR #161) was live but linked from nowhere. Fixed by pointing the signed-out CTAs at `/intro` and restoring an exit affordance on every step; `?from=intro` arrivals still skip the land step.

Shipped August 18, 2026 via PR [#192](https://github.com/jaybalmer/lineage/pull/192) (`ba4cb92`, BUG-161 / BUG-162; branch `fix/signed-out-catalog-add-gate-BUG-161-162`; attended session; no migration; tsc clean):
- BUG-161: every signed-out catalog add silently no-opped on all six entity types. Each `addUser*` action in `lineage-store.ts` bailed with `if (!isAuthUser(...)) return true` before the network call, so the UI reported success for a write it never attempted. Fixed by gating all six add affordances behind a sign-in prompt (gate now, capture-and-replay parked as a separate feature brief).
- BUG-162: the "+ Add to my profile" CTA on board and place detail pages rendered unconditionally and sent signed-out visitors to the riders list. Fixed at the CTA, not in `ownProfilePath()`: signed out, the CTA now ends at sign-in.

Shipped August 17, 2026 via PR [#191](https://github.com/jaybalmer/lineage/pull/191) (`7e400df`, BUG-159; branch `fix/stack-slug-sync-BUG-159`; attended session; migration `20260817000001_public_slug_aliases.sql` applied SAFE additive, plus a GATED cy_1 backfill applied in-session with Jay's approval):
- BUG-159: `profiles.public_slug` was minted once and frozen while person timeline links derive from the live `display_name`, so a renamed member's Stack URL read as a different person (cy_1 resolving to a Stack view titled Cory_Yip). Fix adds `PATCH /api/me/display-name`, which writes the name and re-mints the slug via a new `resyncPublicSlug` helper; the outgoing slug is reserved in the additive `public_slug_aliases` table and `/t/[slug]` 308-redirects old links to the current slug, mirroring the `/people` `person_slug_aliases` pattern. `slugTaken` now treats an aliased slug as reserved, so a re-minted slug is never handed to another owner (this is the half that closes the namespace-squatting risk). Verified on prod: `/t/cory_yip` 308s to `/t/cy_1`, `/t/cy_1` returns 200, both post-deploy assertions pass (0 enabled-null-slug, 0 alias/live collisions). Only cy_1 needed backfill; Cy 2 was already in sync. One path not exercised headlessly: a live signed-in rename could not fire the modal's React click events in the preview harness, so the save-triggered re-mint is verified by type-check plus the resolver test rather than a live rename. **Worth a two-minute manual rename on a test account to close that gap, especially before the public-Stack default flip.**

Shipped August 4, 2026 via PR [#188](https://github.com/jaybalmer/lineage/pull/188) (`6156e2e`, BUG-154 / BUG-157 / BUG-138; branch `fix/p1-owner-curated-private-stories-trust`; attended P1 batch, three P1s in one PR, taken on the manual-session rule; no schema change, no `_public` view touched, no data repair):
- BUG-154: the owner's own `/people/[id]` showed none of the curated member layer (statement, milestones, featured rail) or stat header a logged-out visitor saw. Root cause was NOT the brief's read (that `OwnerTimelinePanel` omits `MemberCuratedSections`; it renders it at `owner-timeline-panel.tsx:773`). The real fault: the person object handed to it was built from `getPersonById` (mock, null for auth users) plus `profileOverride`, neither carrying `membership_tier`, so the paid gate resolved to free and the block returned null. Fix merges the live tier off the store, matching the public branch at `people/[id]/page.tsx:221`. Client-only.
- BUG-157: a member who saved a story "Only me" watched it vanish from the Stories index after refresh. The row was never lost (prod showed the story intact, photo-only with a null title, which is allowed by design). GET `/api/stories` carried an author's non-public rows only on an author-scoped list (the BUG-106 carve-out), so every other list fell to public-only and dropped the author's own story. Jay overrode the brief's Decision 2: every list a signed-in viewer requests now includes their OWN non-public rows (author_id pinned to the viewer, nobody else's visibility changes), and StoryCard badges them "Only you". Archived-author exclusion now runs on both list paths with the viewer exempt.
- BUG-138: trusting an asserter toasted "future tags auto-approve" but trust was retroactive only; `insertTagEvent` never read `tag_trust`. It now checks trust for any tag that would otherwise wait and writes it approved with the subject as decider, gated on `defaultStatus === 'pending'` so editor and system tags pay no extra read; both pair helpers route through it. NOT device-verified this run (the environment safety classifier blocked minting a real session), and prod currently holds zero `tag_trust` rows, so the fix is unobservable until someone trusts a rider again; if a fresh trust does not appear in `/me/settings/trust`, the POST /api/me/trust path is the next thing to look at. Brief archived (`2026-08-01-owner-timeline-milestones.md`, `2026-08-03-private-story-owner-loss.md`, `2026-07-04-trust-auto-approve.md`).
- BUG-153 (closed by PR [#187](https://github.com/jaybalmer/lineage/pull/187), not separately built): the #188-session diagnosis found the stuck feed claim `b5794cf6` (rode_at Whistler Blackcomb, asserted by CY 1, subject CY 2) has a `tag_event` at `status='approved'` with `subject_id = CY 2`, which is exactly the row PR #187's new `declineOwnTagsForClaim` branch moves. The DELETE that used to 403 now returns 200 `{declined:1}` and the claim leaves CY 2's view. Closed as fixed by #187; brief archived (`2026-07-31-feed-claim-delete.md`).

Shipped August 3, 2026 via three attended human bug-fix sessions merged to `main` after the 04:06 triage (reconciled by the August 3 morning digest):

PR [#185](https://github.com/jaybalmer/lineage/pull/185) (`a63d99a`, `fix/compare-avatars-black-BUG-024-067`; the ship-finish of the built-but-unpushed `f47184b`, rebased onto current main, pushed and merged; client-only, no migration):
- BUG-024: three /compare avatar sites (picker button, picker dropdown, side-by-side column headers) drew their own avatar as `bg-[#1C1917]` with `text-foreground` initials, so in light theme the near-black initials went dark-on-dark and each avatar read as a solid black disc. All three now use the shared RiderAvatar (explicit inline colors); the two duplicate local `initials()` helpers were removed. Verified in-browser before/after; zero remaining `rgb(28,25,23)` discs on a full DOM sweep.
- BUG-067: the compare name resolver had no `person` case, so ghost/catalog/duplicate person nodes fell through `getEntityName` (which knows only profiles + mock-data) and rendered "Unknown". Added a `person` case resolving `display_name` from `catalog.people` first, with the default branch falling back the same way. Cleared the hard prerequisite for the compare functional pass (BUG-120 + BUG-123). Brief archived (`2026-06-14-compare-avatars-black.md`).

PR [#186](https://github.com/jaybalmer/lineage/pull/186) (`c65f45e`, `fix/tags-entry-scroll-BUG-150`; the auto-merge-safe pipeline lead, hand-run this morning; client-only, no migration):
- BUG-150: entering `/me/tags` landed at a remembered scroll position with the "Your tags" header, the four status chips and the MeSubNav row above the viewport. Fix is a mount-time `window.scrollTo(0, 0)` in MeTagsPage placed above the render guards so it fires regardless of auth state, covering App Router back-nav restoration, iOS Safari bfcache, and a stale body-scroll-lock restore at once. The MeSubNav horizontal-centering effect (PR #92) is untouched. Optional hardening (a pathname guard on `use-body-scroll-lock`'s `savedScrollY`) was declined and noted for if the symptom shows on another surface. Brief archived (`2026-07-07-tags-entry-scroll.md`).

PR [#187](https://github.com/jaybalmer/lineage/pull/187) (`ef27230`, `fix/connection-token-farm-BUG-151-148`; attended P1 session, two P1s shipped together as the creation and removal sides of one cross-member connection model; no schema change; one GATED claw-back applied in-session on Jay's go-ahead):
- BUG-151: the add-connection popover awarded a contribution token on every picker tap and kept it after the subject declined. Diagnosis found CY 1 wrote 28 third-party claims and the subject declined 27, so 26 tokens sat on rejected claims because `/api/me/tags` never called a reversal. Decision 1 was overridden against the brief to reverse-on-decline (prod showed award-on-approval would zero out most legitimate contributions: of 236 member tags, 57% still pending, only 8% approved). Code: `reverseClaimAwardOnDecline()` + `claimIdsForTagEvents()` in `src/lib/tag-events.ts`, called from both decide routes; only CLAIM tags reverse and only when no pending/approved tag_event remains, so one decliner cannot erase an award still standing for another person on the same row. Also fixed the `selected={[]}` SearchPicker no-op-looking taps. Claw-back: 26 contribution tokens reversed on CY 1's fully-declined claims (balance 104 -> 78, ledger sum == stored balance).
- BUG-148: `DELETE /api/claims/[id]` admitted only asserter-or-editor, so a subject removing a claim asserted ABOUT them got a 403 and the optimistic Remove bounced. A hard delete would be wrong (anyone tagged could destroy another member's row), so a non-asserter caller now has their own tag_events on that claim declined instead (recorded as 'preference', subject as decider); callers with no tag still 403. Verified end-to-end against prod on a local dev server. Brief archived (`2026-07-05-delete-event-blocked.md`).


Shipped July 28, 2026 via PR [#165](https://github.com/jaybalmer/lineage/pull/165) (`240aaf6`, "BUG-122: no-store on GET /api/stories so deleted stories can't return from cache", branch `fix/stories-no-store-cache-BUG-122`; human diagnosis-first session run July 27 evening, merged by Jay July 28; no migration; reconciled same-day by the July 28 triage follow-up):
- BUG-122: the "deleted stories reappeared" report was NOT latent DB rows; read-only prod queries verified the database clean across all three reporter accounts (zero authored stories, no matching title/body rows, no rider tags). Root cause: GET `/api/stories` returned viewer-specific mutable data (own private stories via the ownAuthorList branch, plus viewer_reaction) with no Cache-Control header, so a stale browser/CDN-cached response on the reporter's phone could resurface a since-deleted story. Fix adds `Cache-Control: no-store, max-age=0` (closes the HTTP-cache path; Safari bfcache is out of scope). No data cleanup needed, PR #136 own-author branch untouched. Brief archived (`2026-07-03-deleted-stories-reappear.md`).

Shipped June 17, 2026 via PR [#93](https://github.com/jaybalmer/lineage/pull/93) (`3687549`, `bugfix/bug066-rode-with-dedup`, human-run P1):
- BUG-066: timeline and `/people/[id]` showed many identical undated "Rode with" rider cards for the same rider. Two root causes fixed: the companion fold keyed on year-only `(subject|start|end)` collapsed every same-year visit to one key and gave up at 2+ rode_at, leaking each companion `rode_with` as a standalone card; and every non-board predicate plain-inserted, so repeat tags piled up. Added `claims.parent_claim_id` (text, matches `claims.id`) + rebuilt `claims_public` (the `SELECT c.*` column freeze). The modal stamps `parent_claim_id` on per-ride companion rows and upserts ONE crew `rode_with` per person with a widening year range (client-side, mirroring the board upsert); `/api/claims` adds the same NULL-parent crew upsert as the stale-state backstop. Read path folds by the explicit parent link with a guarded legacy date-key fallback that will not absorb crew rows. `connection-summary` prefers the crew row for the `rode_with` window. No node merge (prod confirmed one real Cory + a separate real "Cy 2" account).
  - **MIGRATION GATE: confirm applied.** PR carries migration `20260617000001` (run-before-merge) + a discovery-first backfill runbook `docs/bug066-rode-with-backfill.sql` (parent the Jay->Sean 1986 companions; collapse the Cy 2->Cory 2026 standalone dups with their paired tag_events). Because `/api/claims` and the modal now send `parent_claim_id` unconditionally, the column must exist in prod or claim inserts fail (PGRST204); if any claim-add is erroring, the migration has not been applied yet. The backfill is what clears the EXISTING duplicate cards; without it the code stops NEW duplicates but the historical Cory/Sean dups remain until the runbook is run.

Shipped June 16, 2026 via PR [#84](https://github.com/jaybalmer/lineage/pull/84) (`92a7ba5`, `bug-059-story-connections-add-new`):
- BUG-059: the Story Connections "+ Connect" / "I was there" popover could only link existing entities; it had no "create a new rider/place/event" action when the search returned no match, unlike the Add Story modal. Lifted the AddStoryModal pattern in: each SearchPicker now passes `onAddNew`/`addNewLabel` and an `addingEntity` state drives a stacked `AddEntityModal`, so a member creates and connects a brand-new entity in one flow (all three types). To avoid racing the connect POST against the catalog write, `addUserPlace/Board/Org/Event/Series` now return a Promise and `AddEntityModal` awaits persistence before firing `onAdded`. No migration.

Shipped June 16, 2026 via PR [#85](https://github.com/jaybalmer/lineage/pull/85) (`dee26a6`, `bug-060-tag-visibility-definer`):
- BUG-060: riders added to an event via "+Add rider" (and any claim that tags another person) appeared then vanished on refresh; the story path persisted. Root cause: `claims_public` / `story_riders_public` are `security_invoker` and LEFT JOIN `tag_events`, which has RLS enabled with no policies, so anon/authenticated readers see zero tag_events rows; under `security_invoker` the join runs as the caller, `te.status` resolves NULL for every row, and the visibility WHERE collapses to "tag_event_id IS NULL only". Self-claims survived; tag-of-another-person claims got a paired tag_event and disappeared. Fix: keep the views `security_invoker` and move only the tag-status check into a `SECURITY DEFINER` helper `tag_event_publicly_visible(uuid)` (no tag_events rows exposed). Migration only, no app code change.
  - **MIGRATION GATE: CLOSED (June 17).** Migration `20260616000001` (the `_public` view / RLS change) is APPLIED in prod, confirmed by Jay: `tag_event_publicly_visible` exists as SECURITY DEFINER, and both `claims_public` and `story_riders_public` are defined to call it. The gate is closed; BUG-060 is fully live.

Shipped June 11, 2026 via PR [#55](https://github.com/jaybalmer/lineage/pull/55) (follow-up task from session 1, no BUG-ID):
- Silent dead writes in the store: updateClaim, removeRidingDay, and updateRidingDay built Supabase queries that never executed (lazy builders, no await), so claim edits and riding-day edits/deletes silently did not persist. Also added PATCH/DELETE /api/claims/[id] so non-editor members can edit and delete their own claims (the old path 403'd through the editor-gated /api/admin).

Shipped June 11, 2026 via PR [#56](https://github.com/jaybalmer/lineage/pull/56) (bug-fix session 2, tag-derived connections):
- BUG-014: tag-derived relationships were invisible on Compare and Connections. Two causes, both fixed: (1) both surfaces read claims subject-only, so a rode_with where the person is the object scored nothing; reads are now symmetric (subject OR object), the Connections incoming read is kept out of dbClaims so no foreign claim leaks onto the user's own timeline, and shared-event matching now counts spectated_at and organized_at. (2) nothing consumed story tags; a new src/lib/connection-derived.ts derives overlap from story_riders_public plus story linked_place_id / linked_event_id plus story_places / story_events through the _public views (co-tag +8 cap +16, story event +10, story place +2), fed to the scorer as a new extraFacts param. No paired claims are written, so decline / disable / per-subject gating propagate for free through the views. Verified in prod by SQL: Cory's approved member-to-member case (Q1) and Sean Balmer's two co-tagged stories (Q2) both derive. Data note, not a code bug: the "86 North American Championships" story has no event entity linked (linked_event_id null, no story_events row), so the +10 event overlap stays dark until the event is linked to the story.
- BUG-023: the Rode With quick-add on /people flipped to a success checkmark on optimistic insert. A member rode_with always lands a pending tag_event, so the control now shows an amber pending clock and fires a plain toast ("Request sent. Pending their approval."); self-only claims (board / place / event / org) keep the checkmark. Verified: the quick-add writes the claim subject = the asserter (me), object = the tagged rider.

Shipped June 11, 2026 via PR [#57](https://github.com/jaybalmer/lineage/pull/57) (bug-fix session 3, catalog-create + event-date cluster):
- BUG-027: a new shop added on the brands page never surfaced in the list. The shop persisted fine; the brands list had no render bucket for `org_type === 'shop'`, so it was filtered out of the view. Fixed by surfacing shops on the brands list and reconciling the header count.
- BUG-028: the nested "add new" catalog create modal opened behind the parent panel and never persisted. Root cause was a z-50 vs z-50 stacking collision where the parent panel's backdrop painted over the earlier-in-DOM create modal. Fixed by raising the nested create modal above its parent. Two reports (June 10 story add-connections flow, June 11 Edit Story Links tab event add); the June 11 14:39 UTC report predated this merge by ~15 hours, so it shipped as a pre-fix duplicate.
- BUG-031: event dates rendered "NaN" and "undefined" on the profile event picker and the event detail header. Two local date formatters assumed a full YYYY-MM-DD; partial dates produced NaN day and undefined month. Fixed with a precision-aware formatter routed through both call sites, preserving event-range compression.

Shipped June 12, 2026 via PR [#60](https://github.com/jaybalmer/lineage/pull/60) (bug-fix session 4, P2 batch subset):
- BUG-026: place photo-upload Save button overflowed the edit-panel frame on a short mobile viewport. Fixed by keeping the action row inside the container with safe-area padding.
- BUG-030: event Edit button was shown to non-editors. Resolved with a creator-or-editor gate: members can edit events they added, others cannot, and the event PATCH enforces it server-side (so the original "could a non-editor save?" question is closed without escalating to P1).
- BUG-032: board model years were doubled in the WHICH BOARD? picker. Fixed by de-duplicating model years before render.
- Note: Session 4 shipped only this subset of the planned P2 batch. BUG-016, 017, 018, 019, 020, 021, 024 (plus the BUG-025 carve-out and the later BUG-033 / 034 / 035) remain open in the Queue.

Shipped June 12, 2026 via PR [#61](https://github.com/jaybalmer/lineage/pull/61) (token earn model + equity offer):
- BUG-012: token balance was static at 200 and did not reward activity. Shipped the contribution-earning model, a daily-visit reward, founding member-token accrual, and the equity-offer repositioning; all merged to main and deployed. Moves BUG-012 out of Deferred.

Shipped June 13, 2026 via PR [#63](https://github.com/jaybalmer/lineage/pull/63) (`380e641`, separate agent):
- BUG-036: admin grant double-logging created phantom `token_events` ledger rows. The grant path was not idempotent (balance overwrite while the ledger insert appended), so a double-clicked admin Grant produced duplicate rows for one intended grant; prod-seen on Cory Yip (two `founder +100 admin_grant` rows 30s apart). Now relevant because `token_events` is the equity-distribution ledger feeding the Sept 2026 share calc. Fixed on the admin grant path.

Shipped June 13, 2026 via PR [#64](https://github.com/jaybalmer/lineage/pull/64) (`86955e3`, "fix(memberships): assign founding numbers by max+1 and clear on downgrade"):
- BUG-037: `founding_member_number` was reused / could collide (assigned by `count(founding)+1` and never cleared on downgrade; prod-seen on Cory Yip keeping #3 after switching founding to lifetime). Fix: new `src/lib/memberships.ts` `nextFoundingMemberNumber()` = `max(founding_member_number)+1` over all profiles (one source of truth, replacing two drifting `count+1` sites); the memberships route's tier block now assigns on founding grant and clears number + badge on any move off founding (overriding a stale value echoed back by the edit form); the Stripe webhook switched to `max+1`; admin `quickGrant` stopped computing the number client-side; gift-redeem clears founding fields on the annual downgrade. Follows BUG-036 / PR #63 (same admin grant path).
  - Carve-out (Jay decision, not a regression; next free id BUG-039 if elevated): two of three acceptance lines fully met (numbers unique / never reused, downgrade releases the number). The third, "concurrent signups do not collide," is only partially met: `max+1` is a read-then-write, so two simultaneous founding grants could still read the same max. Shipped deliberately code-only, no migration. To fully close, the follow-up is a dedicated Postgres sequence (or DB-side atomic assignment) for `founding_member_number`. Low urgency: founding is a small, manual, launch-time cohort. Logged here rather than reopening BUG-037.
  - Data note (no action required): clearing is forward-looking. A pre-existing non-founding profile still holding a stale founding number (beyond Cory, manually cleared June 13) is not auto-scrubbed, but `max+1` reads the whole column so it cannot cause a collision. A one-line `UPDATE` can scrub them if desired.

Shipped June 13, 2026 via PR [#65](https://github.com/jaybalmer/lineage/pull/65) (`28914aa`, bug-fix session, Profile + timeline trio):
- BUG-035: the rich profile summary card (background, profile picture, identity, BOARDS / PLACES / EVENTS stat tiles) rendered only for the signed-in owner. Now the summary card shows for public visitors and non-owner members too, with private fields still respected (the expected owner-vs-public stat-count difference for private / pending claims is unchanged).
- BUG-034: the profile summary stat tiles were static. Each Boards / Places / Events tile now applies the same category filter (and view) as its matching filter pill.
- BUG-033: single-date claims rendered a spurious "to present" range on timeline cards. The label now shows just the date for a single-date / point-in-time entry; "to present" appears only for genuinely ongoing claims. Both product decisions in the brief were taken (strip "present" globally for non-ongoing entries; hide precise location for non-owners).
- The three June 12 reports that match this PR but predate the merge shipped as pre-fix duplicates.

Shipped June 14, 2026 via PR [#70](https://github.com/jaybalmer/lineage/pull/70) (`74bad01`, `auto/bugfix-20260614-1142`, first autonomous auto-bugfix-pipeline ship):

Master-only trim, mute toggle unchanged. Shared by all three play surfaces. Pure client, no migration.

Shipped June 15, 2026 via PR [#72](https://github.com/jaybalmer/lineage/pull/72) (`b49f18a`, `auto/bugfix-20260615-0500`, autonomous auto-bugfix-pipeline, auto-merged at 05:03 PT):
- BUG-042: members could not add a board brand on `/snowboarding/brands` (the "Failed to save brand" toast). `addUserOrg` still POSTed to the editor-gated `/api/admin`, so a non-editor got a 403 and the optimistic insert rolled back. Repointed `addUserOrg` to the member-allowed `/api/catalog/entity` route with `type: "org"` (matching `addUserPlace` / `addUserBoard`), and added an org branch to that route that whitelists the org fields, stamps `community_status='unverified'` and `added_by` server-side, dedupes on name, and awards a contribution token. No migration, no `_public` view change. Pipeline ran the BUG-042 lead brief this triage left, tsc-gated, judged "safe", opened and auto-merged PR #72.

Shipped June 15, 2026 via PR [#73](https://github.com/jaybalmer/lineage/pull/73) (`82865d8`, P1 batch: mobile story menu + comment-email reliability):
- BUG-044: the story owner edit/delete (3-dots) trigger was `opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100`, invisible on touch. First fix gated the hover-reveal behind `(hover: hover)`, but under Tailwind v4 that collided with the wrapping `group-hover` and hid the control on desktop too; the same-branch follow-up removed the opacity gating so the owner-only control is always visible (muted, darkens on hover) and extended the treatment to the claim-card, day-card, post-card, day-post-card, and the editor remove-rider control. The non-owner quick-claim affordance stays hover-gated.
- BUG-045 (reliability half): `fireCommentNotification` committed the 6h batch window before confirming the Resend send, so a failed send silently suppressed comment emails for 6h; the window now commits only after a confirmed send. The reported Hotmail case is a deliverability / domain-auth root cause (verify SPF/DKIM/DMARC for `linestry.com` in Resend), handed to Jay as a DNS follow-up, not a code fix. The DNS follow-up remains open against BUG-045.

Verified NOT reproducible on current main: the community read is public-only with no `community_id` filter, and 21 of 21 public stories rendered (the 1 private excluded). Closed June 11, 2026 without a separate fix (fixed outside the bug flow):
- BUG-029: newly added riders landing as Catalog instead of Unclaimed. Logged from a June 10 18:05 report that predated the PR #54 deploy by about an hour and described the Chris Fulton row created on the old code, which session 1 already repaired to unclaimed. The underlying cause (ghost creation in the store's addUserPerson, not POST /api/claims) was fixed in PR #54 to write node_status='unclaimed' explicitly. June 11 prod audit confirmed zero member-added riders filed as catalog and zero people rows created since the deploy, so there was no reproducing case and nothing to build. Closed as a pre-deploy duplicate of BUG-022's residual.

## Claude Code mini-brief format

Each cluster in the Queue carries a short brief in this shape so it is hand-off ready:

```
### BUG-NNN: <short title>  [Pn] [reproducible|needs-info]
- Reported: <date(s)>, by <who>, <count> reports
- Symptom: <what the user sees>
- Repro: <steps, or "needs-info: <what is missing>">
- Surface: <page/route, e.g. /snowboarding/profile>
- Suspected area: <best guess at the file/component/route, from CLAUDE.md knowledge>
- Session replay: <PostHog link if available, else timestamp + user to find it>
- Acceptance: <how we know it is fixed>
```

Notes for whoever drafts the brief: apply the standing no-em-dash rule. Pull suspected-area guesses from the codebase CLAUDE.md (stack, routes, gotchas) so Claude Code starts warm. When a fix ships, move the item to **Shipped** with the PR number.
